---
type: log
---
A dated record of every ingest, query, health check, verification, schema change, and tool change, and what I approved, revised, or rejected. Newest entries are at the top, so a new entry goes just under the line below, above the newest one. Past entries are never rewritten. This file holds November 2026; each month has its own file in `_logs/`. Format and rules: `_AGENTS.md` and [[SCHEMA - Index and Log#The log]]. Agents: don't read this file whole; read only the first 15 or so lines to add an entry, and search the headings.

---

### [2026-11-23] tool | Nicrosil 3.29.0: the atlas and the Lens

- **Think:** Harrison found the wiki map unappealing, laggy and not very helpful, and asked for other ways to see the whole wiki. From four mockups (topic atlas, neighborhood lens, bridge matrix, review mosaic) he chose the topic atlas as the main map and the lens for single pages, folded into the relations panel. He wanted the topics to be a list he can edit and the agent checks at each ingest, with Nicrosil redrawing the atlas from it automatically rather than the agent keeping it up, to save tokens. Both the Hierarchy and Map views were to go.
- **Calculate:** Claude (claude-opus-5-5) rebuilt both views in `main.js` and `styles.css` (details in [[Nicrosil Changelog]]):
  - **Atlas** (`Alt+M`): one circle per topic in the new file [[_topics]], bands between topics as wide as the links joining them, a dot per page in `Wiki/` and `Perspectives/`. Each page's topic is worked out from the links, spreading out from each topic's anchor pages (typed links count double), so nothing is stored on the pages. The layout is computed once and kept until links or topics change: no live physics, no animated arrows. Click a topic to zoom in, a dot to trace its links; search, status or type colors, and a side panel with counts, anchors, most-linked pages, linked and unlinked topics, and pages almost in another topic.
  - **Lens** (`Alt+L`, the relations panel renamed): plain links as well as typed ones, the page's topic (a click shows it in the atlas), "two steps away, not linked yet" suggestions, and a trail of recent pages.
  - Removed: the Hierarchy tree, the force-directed Map and their settings and styles. New command **Show this page in the atlas**. Dashboard card renamed **Atlas**.
  - [[_topics]] created with 12 topics seeded from the earlier link-cluster analysis (the 13 clusters, with "Neural Network Internals" widened to "How AI Works" and "Cold War Policy" folded into "Covert Action & Ethics"), each with three to five anchors. The names and anchors are Claude's first draft for Harrison to edit.
  - Version 3.28.0 → 3.29.0 in `manifest.json` and [[Nicrosil]]; the guide's relations panel and wiki map sections are rewritten as The Lens and The atlas, and the guide is back to `draft` with four change lines.
  - Tested in a browser against the vault's real pages and links with a stand-in for Obsidian's API (whole map, topic zoom, tracing, search, Escape, wheel zoom, a missing anchor, no topic file, the Lens and its Enlarge window). Not yet seen in Obsidian itself.
  - Not changed: the health-check script still counts links from `_topics.md` toward a page's inbound links. Only anchors are linked there, and they are the most-linked pages anyway.
- **Verify:**
  - Pending: Harrison to try the atlas and the Lens in Obsidian, edit [[_topics]], and re-review [[Nicrosil]] (listed changes only)

### [2026-11-23] schema | A topic list for the atlas, checked at each ingest (version 11.0)

- **Think:** With the atlas drawn from a topic list, Harrison asked that the list be his to edit and that the agent check and update it at every ingest.
- **Calculate:** Claude (claude-opus-5-5) changed two rule files:
  - [[SCHEMA]] (Folder layout, Files that are not wiki pages, The other files): lists `_topics.md` and who keeps it.
  - [[SCHEMA - Ingest]] (Dissect and draft): new step 6, Check the topic list. The agent reads `_topics.md` after drafting; adds a topic only for a new subject of about five pages or a new course, or an anchor when a new page is central to a topic with fewer than six; proposes renames, merges, splits and removals instead of making them. Source card becomes step 7. Report section C ends with the topic list's changes.
  - [[SCHEMA]] version 10.0 → 11.0 (a workflow change); line added to [[Schema Changelog]]. Both files set to `draft` with `verified_on` kept and `changes_since_verified` lines (one on SCHEMA, three on Ingest).
- **Verify:**
  - Pending: re-review of [[SCHEMA]] and [[SCHEMA - Ingest]], listed changes only

### [2026-11-23] schema | Link first mentions everywhere, and backfill links to new pages (version 10.0)

- **Think:** After the linking audit (same-ingest mentions ~94% linked, new → older pages ~77%, older → newer pages ~58%, People pages 51%), Harrison asked for two changes: link the first mention of every page, not only glossary terms, and add a backfill step to ingest. He said backfill may set verified pages back to `draft`, as long as each change is listed in `changes_since_verified`, since he can re-verify those quickly when he knows exactly what changed.
- **Calculate:** Claude (claude-opus-5-5) changed two rule files:

### [2026-11-22] schema | Nicrosil keeps the index up to date (8.10)

- **Think:** Harrison wants Nicrosil to maintain `_index.md` itself, so agents spend no tokens reading or editing it.

It runs when Obsidian opens and a few seconds after each change; there is an **Update the index now** command and a **Keep the index up to date** setting; an automatic run that would remove more than 20 or add more than 100 entries stops with a notice. Tested against the real index (436 pages: no change when in sync, and add, delete, rename, move, fill, new section, duplicate and CRLF cases). ### [2026-11-19] schema | Rules split into AGENTS.md and SCHEMAS/ (7.0)

- **Think:** Every agent had to read the whole SCHEMA (about 11,600 tokens) before any task, and ingests also read all of `index.md` (about 10,500) and sometimes the log (about 45,000). I asked for the efficiency plan in full: split the rules into files in a new `SCHEMAS/` folder, make `AGENTS.md` the only file read every time and use it as a directory, split the log by month, and the rest.
- **Calculate:** Claude (claude-opus-5-5):
  - `AGENTS.md` rewritten as the start page: scope, drafts only, permissions, voices, no unsourced claims, the index line, the log entry format (with the new `tool` operation), the commit format, token-saving rules, and a table of which rule file each task needs. `CLAUDE.md` added; it loads `AGENTS.md` for Claude Code.
  - `SCHEMA.md` moved to `SCHEMAS/` as the overview. The rules moved, wording kept, to [[SCHEMA - Writing Pages]], [[SCHEMA - Review]], [[SCHEMA - Ingest]] (which now holds the steps that were in [[Dissect and Ingest a Source]]), [[SCHEMA - Query]], [[SCHEMA - Health Check]], [[SCHEMA - Index and Log]], [[SCHEMA - Changes]], and [[SCHEMA - Export]]; the changelog is [[Schema Changelog]], with a 7.0 line. Each rule file is `draft` with `changes_since_verified` lines: the moves, plus every real rule change.
  - New rules: never read the whole log; find pages by searching names and aliases; read only your batch of the Ingest Queue; the log is one file per month (`Logs/Log YYYY-MM.md`); `tool` operation for `Tools/` and Nicrosil; health checks run the new script and by default read only pages changed since the last report plus a sample of 20; finished queue batches collapse into a Done section.
  - The old root `SCHEMA.md` is now a short "moved" stub; Nicrosil 2.6.0 moves it to the trash at its next start.
  - [[Dissect and Ingest a Source]] cut to its five inputs (verified page, so back to `draft` with a note). [[Triage]] header updated and a Done section added. `log.md` header (not an entry) updated. Templates and the Health Check Report template point to the new files.
  - Mechanical link fixes (status unchanged): links to the old SCHEMA sections in [[Setting Up Git for the Coppermind]], [[Choosing a Claude Model for Coppermind Work]] (its footnote 11 covered both health checks and queries, so queries now cite a new footnote 14), [[Analysis of Competing Hypotheses]], [[Intelligence Community Style Summaries]], and [[SRC - Puzzles and Mysteries (Slides)]]. Older log entries and health check reports keep their old section links, since they are records.
  - Estimated rules read per task: a query about 12,000 → 2,000 tokens; an ingest about 24,400 → 8,300 (68,600 → 8,300 when the log was read).
- **Verify:**
  - Re-review: [[SCHEMA]], [[SCHEMA - Writing Pages]], [[SCHEMA - Review]], [[SCHEMA - Ingest]], [[SCHEMA - Query]], [[SCHEMA - Health Check]], [[SCHEMA - Index and Log]], [[SCHEMA - Changes]], [[SCHEMA - Export]], [[Dissect and Ingest a Source]]
  - Read `AGENTS.md` as a whole (it has no properties; its changes are noted on [[SCHEMA]])
  - Change this project's instruction in claude.ai from "read the SCHEMA file first" to "read AGENTS.md first"
- **Commit:** `schema: 7.0 split rules into AGENTS.md and SCHEMAS/, monthly log, health check script`

### [2026-11-19] schema | Nicrosil 2.5.2 properties strip in hover previews

- **Think:** When I point at a link, the pop-up preview showed the full Properties block, while an open page shows the one-line strip. I asked for the preview to look like a normal page.
- **Calculate:** Nothing to compute. The preview is not a normal tab, so the plugin notes which page the pointer last hovered a link to (Obsidian's `hover-link` event), and when a preview pop-up appears within a few seconds it treats the pop-up like a page: the full block is hidden, and the same strip is put in its place. Clicking the strip in the preview opens the real block. Not checked in Obsidian yet.
- **Changed:**
  - `.obsidian/plugins/nicrosil/main.js`, `manifest.json` (2.5.1 to 2.5.2): the strip code now works on any container, not only an open tab, and watches new hover previews; the Dashboard, index and log are left alone; the watchers are dropped when a preview closes and when the plugin unloads
  - [[Nicrosil]]: the properties strip section, version 2.5.2 and history line; the guide was verified, so it is back to `draft` with a `changes_since_verified` note (§7 rule 8)
- **Verify:**
  - Guide needs verifying again
  - Reload Nicrosil, then point at a link in a page (for example a term in the AI Deception summary)
- **Commit:** `schema: Nicrosil 2.5.2 properties strip in hover previews`

  - Map: dots are lit orbs (a soft gradient per page type) with a halo, hub halos breathe slowly, dots fade in one after another when the map opens, the arrows and their traveling lights are slightly stronger, hovered arrows glow in their relationship color, labels are crisper
  - Hierarchy: cards have a gradient fill and a teal (verified) or copper (draft) glow, the page-type dot glows, branches glow in their relationship color with lights running from parent to child, cards and branches fade in from left to right, the open/close circles glow and grow on hover, hovering a card brightens its whole path

### [2026-11-19] schema | Nicrosil 2.4.9 clickable change lines (SCHEMA 6.2)

- **Think:** I asked for a line in the Changed since verified list to take me to that spot in the page when I click it.
- **Calculate:** A change note is free text, so it has no position of its own. I chose a small convention: the line may end with a link to the section it changed, `[[#Heading]]`. Existing notes have no link, and most of them (the citation move on 62 pages) touch the whole page, so they still just open the page.
- **Changed:**
  - `.obsidian/plugins/nicrosil/main.js`, `styles.css`, `manifest.json` (2.4.8 to 2.4.9): a change line with a `[[#Heading]]` (or `[[#^block]]`) becomes a link, with a small copper "→ Heading" tag, on the review card and on the Dashboard's Changed since verified list; clicking goes to that heading in the page (the open tab if there is one), `Ctrl+click` opens a new tab; if the heading no longer exists the page opens with a notice; a line with no link behaves as before
  - [[SCHEMA]] 6.2 (still `draft`, one more `changes_since_verified` line): §7 rule 8 describes the `[[#Heading]]` ending and says to keep the line in quotes; changelog line 6.2; `version: 6.2`. The two lines already in its note now carry links, so they work as a first test.
  - `AGENTS.md`: the change-note instruction asks for the `[[#Heading]]` ending when the change is in one section.
  - [[Nicrosil]]: the review queue bullet, version 2.4.9 and history line; the guide was a re-review draft already, so it gained one more `changes_since_verified` line
- **Verify:**
  - Pending my review: SCHEMA 6.2 and the Nicrosil guide
  - Reload Nicrosil, then click a line on the SCHEMA's card (it should go to its section)
- **Commit:** `schema: Nicrosil 2.4.9 clickable change lines, SCHEMA 6.2`

### [2026-11-17] schema | Typed links reworked (version 5.0)

- **Think:** After the first health check found 82% of typed links were `related`, I asked why ingest makes so few strong links and where to fix it. I approved the proposed changes, and the clean-up of `related` links stored twice (health check flags 3 and 4).
- **Calculate:** Claude (claude-opus-5-5) found that 157 of the 313 `related` links repeated a link already stored on the other page (140 in 70 two-way pairs, 17 as the reverse of a stronger type), 11 pointed at person pages with no type for authorship, and the rest mostly needed a cause, kind-of, or governing type the schema didn't have. The old §5 rule 3 asked for glossary and concept links in both directions, the Glossary, Person, Summary, Guide, and Prompt templates offered little besides `related`, and nothing made the agent justify a `related` link. Changes:
  - [[SCHEMA]]: §5 rewritten. New types `type_of`, `causes`, and `developed_by`; `applies_to` now covers rules that govern something; `supports` and `contradicts` defined for concept pages; types listed in the order to try them, with `related` last. New rules: store a link on the page the sentence starts with and never the reverse; propose links to verified pages rather than falling back to `related`; "see also" links stay in the page text; every `related` link names the closest type and why it doesn't fit; links rest on what a source says; "Sources disagree" across two pages becomes a `contradicts` proposal; glossary and concept pages link to each other in the text. §4 lists the new properties, §10 adds the typed-link mix to the health check, and a 5.0 line was added to the changelog. `status` set back to `draft` and `verified_on` cleared (§7).
  - `_templates/`: Analysis, Case Study, Concept, Framework, Glossary Term, Guide, Person, Prompt, Summary, and Workflow now carry the same ten typed-link properties, in table order. The `Health Check Report` template has a new row, "Typed-link mix".
  - [[Dissect and Ingest a Source]]: step 2 records the relations a source states; step 4 applies the §5 rules; report section D groups links by type and gives a reason for each `related`. It was verified, so `status` is back to `draft`, old `verified_on` kept (§7).
  - Clean-up: removed 82 repeated `related` links from 47 draft pages (one side of 66 pairs, and 16 reverse copies of a stronger type). No verified page changed. Typed links are now 299: 231 `related`, 49 `part_of`, 7 `example_of`, 7 `applies_to`, 4 `counters`, 1 `supports`.
  - Left for item-by-item review (both pages verified): 4 two-way pairs and Confirmation Bias → related → [[Analysis of Competing Hypotheses]]. Listed in [[Health Check 2026-11-17]], where flags 3 and 4 are now recorded as fixed (9 open).
  - [[_index]]: moved [[Dissect and Ingest a Source]] to Drafts; totals now 54 verified, 182 drafts.
- **Verify:** pending. [[SCHEMA]] and [[Dissect and Ingest a Source]] need re-verifying.
- **Commit:** `schema: typed links reworked (v5.0); remove 82 repeated related links`

### [2026-11-11] setup | Properties strip stacking fix (v0.5.1)

- **Think:** Every time I switched between editing and reading view, another properties strip appeared, stacking up above or below the page title.
- **Calculate:** Claude (claude-opus-5-5) found the cause in the Coppermind Review plugin. When Obsidian re-renders a note, it can re-create the Properties block or move the title. The plugin only checked for a strip directly in front of the Properties block, so the old strip was left stranded and a new one was added each time. The fix removes any strip that isn't directly in front of a Properties block before adding one, so each block keeps exactly one. Version 0.5.0 → 0.5.1. No page content changed.
  - Tested outside Obsidian (jsdom) on both ways a re-render can strand a strip: 6 strips after five switches before the fix, 1 after.
- **Verify:** pending. To reload the plugin and switch a page between editing and reading a few times.
- **Commit:** `plugin: fix stacking properties strips (v0.5.1)`

### [2026-11-11] setup | Review queue, flag resolving, hotkeys, and Home dashboard

- **Think:** The Verify button worked, but reviewing still meant going back to a long list after every page. I asked for: verify-and-next with a skip option, a review sidebar that stays open, a Resolve button on flags, hotkeys and a status-bar counter, and a dashboard on Home. I asked to be told which design choices need my input.
- **Calculate:** Claude (claude-opus-5-5) updated the Coppermind Review plugin to v0.2:
  - **Verify and next:** after Verify or Reject, the next draft in the queue opens (setting, on by default). **Skip** moves a draft to the end of the queue until Obsidian restarts.
  - **Review queue sidebar:** progress bar, a card for the current page with its flags and Verify / Skip / Reject buttons, every draft with flag badges, a queue-order menu (default: clean pages first), and a collapsed list of verified pages.
  - **Resolve flags:** a Resolve button on accuracy-check, gap, question, and warning callouts (Reading view), in the sidebar card, and on `Alt+F`. It asks what I decided, removes the callout, and saves the decision; the decision is written into this log when I verify or reject the page.
  - **Hotkeys and status bar:** `Alt+V` verify, `Alt+R` reject, `Alt+S` skip, `Alt+N` next, `Alt+F` resolve, `Alt+Q` queue, `Ctrl+Enter` to confirm; "X/Y verified" in the status bar opens the queue.
  - **Home dashboard:** a live `coppermind-dashboard` block added to [[_dashboard]] under "Review progress": progress, a table by page type, open flags by kind with links, and drafts older than 14 days ([[SCHEMA#7. Draft vs. verified|Schema §7]], item 7).
  - Rewrote [[Nicrosil]] to cover all of it.
  - Tested outside Obsidian against copies of the SAIL drafts, [[_index]], and this log (flag parsing and removal on every draft, queue order, skip, verify, reject, and log entries). Not yet tried inside Obsidian.
- **Verify:** pending
  - Plugin v0.2: to try a short review session and check the page, [[_index]], and this log after each action
  - [[Nicrosil]]: to verify after I follow it
  - [[_dashboard]]: whether I want the dashboard there
- **Commit:** `setup: review queue, flag resolving, hotkeys, Home dashboard (Coppermind Review v0.2)`

### [2026-11-11] setup | Verify and Reject buttons

- **Think:** I wanted a button on each wiki page that does the logistics of verifying a draft, so reviewing is easier. Verifying still means I checked the page; the button only does the bookkeeping.
- **Calculate:** Claude (claude-sonnet-5-5) wrote a small local plugin, Coppermind Review (`.obsidian/plugins/coppermind-review/`), with a Verify button and a Reject button on every page in `Wiki/`, `Perspectives/`, and `Sources/Source Cards/`. Verify sets `status` and `verified_on`, updates [[_index]], and appends to this log; it warns about open flag callouts and blocks source cards with no reliability rating. Reject requires a reason, logs it, removes the index entry, and deletes the page. Neither button commits to git. Drafted the guide [[Nicrosil]]. Also added one sentence to [[SCHEMA#7. Draft vs. verified|Schema §7]] (items 3 and 6) saying the buttons count as me marking a page verified or rejecting it.
